[jevcode] JevCode is a coding agent where Jev, a decision model, makes every
          decision: what kind of step comes next, which files matter, how risky
          an action is, whether a step worked. In jev-only mode code proposes
          fixes and tests verify them; in jev+llm mode the code model writes the
          code.
[jevcode] Describe the change in plain words and press Enter; a run starts,
          shows every decision, and stops to ask before anything risky. The code
          model writes the code, Jev decides each step.
